# SGW_Beta_Site_Complete_Dev_Master_v1

Markdown rendering of `SGW_Beta_Site_Complete_Dev_Master_v1.xlsx` from the SGW Claude Server Handoff Pack v1.2.
Generated by `xlsx_to_md.py`; cell values only (no formulas, formatting or comments).
Sheet order and headers are preserved verbatim. Evidence labels inside the
sheets control authority, per the pack's `docs/SOURCE_POLICY.md`.

## 00_README

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Workbook | SGW Beta Site — Complete Dev Master v1 |
| Strict scope | Beta Site runtime only. E1/Visit1, Closed-Beta/Visit2 and late E2/Visit3 are separated. Omega Site, Men'Fa, High Council, Harset and other worlds are OFFWORLD blocks; only Beta handoffs/returns are retained. |
| World phases | Beta_Site_Evo_1 World23 = original E1/early Beta; Beta_Site_E2 World60 = late Beta E2. Both have separate cooked Stargate records. |
| Visit structure | 1) first SGU visit during Beta shutdown/relocation; 2) second/Closed-Beta SGU return with Baksheu/Nine-Peaks/Men'Fa chain; 3) late E2 Ra/Straegis emergency where SGU and Praxis jointly operate, followed by Praxis salvage/espionage. |
| SGU factions | SGU Human, Free Jaffa and Asgard all have early Beta content. Their mission families are kept separate. |
| Praxis factions | Coarse progression directly sends Loyalist Jaffa and OP-Core Human to Beta in the later Praxis node. Goa'uld has no confirmed Beta node in the supplied progression. |
| E1 map status | Very strong: main UMAP + 630 streaming UMAP cells + MapData package are indexed, plus E1 minimap and exact Gate6/Kismet. |
| E2 map status | Incomplete: World60, Gate16 and E2 minimap survive, but no named Beta_Site_E2 UMAP/MapData or E2-named Kismet was found in the current handoff. |
| Can we build it? | YES, a complete playable Beta Site reconstruction is realistic with the assets/data we have. NO, it cannot yet be claimed as a 1:1 original reconstruction because E2 map/Kismet and server spawn/grant/task bindings are missing; Visit2 also has placeholder dialogue. |
| Important raw conflict | 1807-1826 form a detailed Closed-Beta/Baksheu rewrite/return chain, while older Asgard/Nine-Peaks missions have overlapping story beats with more complete dialogue. Both are preserved rather than silently merged. |
| Evidence labels | CONFIRMED / SOURCE-BACKED; USER-CONFIRMED; RECONSTRUCTION / INFERENCE; PARTIAL / UNRESOLVED / MISSING. |
| Authority rule | Raw cooked client data outranks progression/secondary sheets. Missing server logic stays visible instead of being guessed. |

_12 data rows, 2 columns._
